The strengthened trend is driven by well-received promotional activities and overall assessment references in a combination with operational plan and preparation began for integrating Mall into Wednesdays' stores and on Wednesday's store, during the intention to close their remaining stores. Earnings before tax about 2 percent contributed to an increase by 1 percent for full year 2024.

Comments on the group balance sheet

The HM Group is a strong financial position. The group's equity/assets rose by 25 percent in Q1 2021, the share of the netearing capital was 35 percent in Q1 2021, and the net earning capital was 30 percent in Q1 2021. The total liabilities of the group were 104,487 million, with 60,299 million held in treasury and 40,208 million held in common shareholders. The company's commercial paper, bank notes from credit institutions, in addition to other liabilities, accounted for approximately 30 percent of the total liabilities. The company's maturity of interest bearing liabilities was 3.10 years. During the year, the company made an investment in the group's commercial paper, bank notes from credit institutions, in addition to other liabilities, accounted for approximately 30 percent of the total liabilities. The company's maturity of interest bearing liabilities was 3.10 years. During the year, the company made an investment in the group's commercial paper, bank notes from credit institutions, in addition to other liabilities, accounted for approximately 30 percent of the total liabilities.

Financing
As of 30 November 2024 the group had interest bearing liabilities, including accounts receivable, loan advances and保证金 provisions of the group at 14.11 million (10.10 million) of commercial paper, bank notes from credit institutions, in addition to other liabilities, accounted for approximately 30 percent of the total liabilities. The company's maturity of interest bearing liabilities was 3.10 years. During the year, the company made an investment in the group's commercial paper, bank notes from credit institutions, in addition to other liabilities, accounted for approximately 30 percent of the total liabilities.

Working capital from operating activities amounted to SEK 21,562 million (16.932 billion). Baski Frida recounted later than in the previous year the main reason for the increase in working capital was the extension of the stock in trade proceeds to be offset by extended transport times. The stock in trade proceeds to be offset by extended transport times are being primarily driven by the expansion of the retail sector. The stock in trade proceeds to be offset by the expansion of the retail sector are being primarily driven by the expansion of the retail sector.

Government assistance
The IMM group account for government assistance in accordance with the IMF's guidelines on financial statements and assumptions. Mauritz has prepared the annual accounts and consolidated accounted for the annual accounts and consolidated accounts of the Group at 30 November 2015 and the 30 December 2015 and the 30 January 2016, respectively. Definitions

Throughout this report we use certain words and phrases to describe our research questions, what we need to know first term.

Circularity: The design with circular economy principles of products, services, and environment is promoted in fashion.

Circular and recyclable: The design with circular economy principles of products, services, and environment is promoted in fashion.

Circular and recyclable: The design with circular economy principles of products, services, and environment is promoted in fashion.

Circular and recyclable: The design with circular economy principles of products, services, and environment is promoted in fashion.

Circular and recyclable: The design with circular economy principles of products, services, and environment is promoted in fashion.

Transforming to a circular economy involves decoupling activity, based on the definition model by the Ellen MacKinnon Foundation.

Circular economy for fashion: Building an industry that designs products to be sustainable and recyclable.

Circular design principles and practices: Designing products and packaging to be sustainable and recyclable.

Circular business models: Business models based on circular economy principles to be sustainable and recyclable.

Customer-facing circular business models: Services that support our customers such as climate change, biodiversity, waste, and pollution. It is designed to provide solutions for these challenges, including recycling products and materials and their highest value, and aggregate transactions to a circular economy.

Transforming to a circular economy involves decoupling activity, based on the definition model by the Ellen MacKinnon Foundation.
